NOTE|info|identity|The 26 pinned section-3 strings are used verbatim in home/away for every row of every season. Rename traps mapped silently to the pinned strings, each mapped once here: Athletic de Bilbao/Athletic Club -> always Ath Bilbao; Atletico de Madrid -> always Ath Madrid (never Atletico); Rayo Vallecano -> always Vallecano; Deportivo Alaves -> Alaves; UD Almeria -> Almeria; CD Leganes -> Leganes; Real Oviedo -> Oviedo; Real Betis Balompie -> Betis; Real Sociedad -> Sociedad; RCD Espanyol -> Espanol; RC Celta (de Vigo) -> Celta; CA Osasuna -> Osasuna; RCD Mallorca -> Mallorca; Cadiz CF -> Cadiz; Real Valladolid -> Valladolid; UD Las Palmas -> Las Palmas; Real Madrid CF -> Real Madrid; FC Barcelona -> Barcelona; Sevilla FC -> Sevilla; Valencia CF -> Valencia; Villarreal CF -> Villarreal; Elche CF -> Elche; Getafe CF -> Getafe; Granada CF -> Granada; Levante UD -> Levante. Source stock names (RSSSF/fbref) map 1:1 per the RSSSF round-by-round short forms (Madrid, Atletico, Athletic, Alaves, Cadiz, Almeria, Leganes, Espanyol...) and fbref forms (Atletico Madrid, Athletic Club, Celta Vigo, Real Betis, Real Sociedad, Rayo Vallecano, Alaves, Real Oviedo). No club changed its identity in-window.
NOTE|info|venue_policy|MATCH stadium/city = per Wikipedia season-article stadium/location tables for 2021-22 .. 2024-25 (second index; RSSSF carries no venues), ASCII-ized; city = the canonical city of the club (EPL-pack precedent; e.g. Ath Madrid/Real Madrid/Vallecano -> Madrid, Betis/Sevilla -> Seville, Espanol -> Barcelona). 2025-26 stadium = the fbref per-match venue label (carrier). Era-stable constants for 24 clubs; documented changes: Barcelona 2021-22 Camp Nou -> 2022-23 Spotify Camp Nou -> 2023-24 Estadi Olimpic Lluis Companys (Montjuic, Camp Nou under renovation) -> 2024-25 Estadi Olimpic Lluis Companys (per the season article) -> 2025-26 three venues per fbref (Estadi Johan Cruyff MD4-MD5, Estadi Olimpic Lluis Companys MD7/MD9/MD11, Spotify Camp Nou from MD13, reopened mid-season); Atletico 2021-22 Wanda Metropolitano -> 2022-23/2023-24 Civitas Metropolitano -> 2024-25 Metropolitano -> 2025-26 Riyadh Air Metropolitano (fbref); Mallorca 2021-22 Visit Mallorca Estadi (Wikipedia; fbref prints "Iberostar Estadi" - resolved to the season-article name per venue policy) -> 2022-23.. Estadi Mallorca Son Moix; Getafe Coliseum Alfonso Perez all seasons (2023-24/2024-25 articles print short "Coliseum"); Celta Estadio Abanca Balaidos; Almeria (2022-23, 2023-24) Power Horse Stadium; Girona Estadi Municipal de Montilivi; Valladolid (2022-23, 2024-25) Estadio Jose Zorrilla; Las Palmas (2023-24, 2024-25) Estadio Gran Canaria; Leganes (2024-25) Estadio Municipal de Butarque; Cadiz Estadio Nuevo Mirandilla (renamed 2021, before the window); Espanyol RCDE Stadium; Osasuna Estadio El Sadar; Sociedad Reale Arena; Sevilla Estadio Ramon Sanchez-Pizjuan; Valencia Estadio de Mestalla; Villarreal Estadio de la Ceramica. 2025-26 specials: Betis played the whole season at Estadio La Cartuja de Sevilla (Benito Villamarin closed for 2030 World Cup works); Rayo played MD24 (vs Ath Madrid) at Estadio Municipal de Butarque (pitch conditions at Vallecas; Wikipedia note [a]); Oviedo at Estadio Nuevo Carlos Tartiere. No neutral-venue league fixtures otherwise.

NOTE|info|continuity|Continuity-clause accounting (gap-free league span): all 38 matchdays of all five seasons exist and are dated; no league fixture was cancelled in the window. Documented disruptions, rows always keep their original MD labels while the file stays date-sorted: 2023-24 round 16 Granada-Athletic was abandoned at 0-1 in the 17th minute (spectator cardiac arrest; RSSSF "abd" line) and the remaining 73 minutes were completed on 2023-12-11 with the final score 1-1 - the pack carries ONE row (2023-12-11 Granada 1-1 Ath Bilbao, MD16) and the abandoned start carries no row, per RSSSF doctrine; 2025-26 MD6 Celta-Betis played 2025-08-27 (Europa League clash; original 20 Sep - Wikipedia rescheduled register); MD16 Levante-Villarreal postponed 14 Dec 2025 (storm) -> played 2026-02-18; MD19 Barcelona-Atletico played 2025-12-02 and Athletic-Real Madrid 2025-12-03 (Supercopa clash; original 11 Jan 2026); MD23 Sevilla-Girona postponed (storm) 7 Feb -> 8 Feb 2026 and Rayo-Oviedo postponed (pitch) 7 Feb -> 4 Mar 2026. Winter breaks are scheduling, not gaps. Season spans as listed under round_counts.

NOTE|info|source_adaptation|WO section-4 design: RSSSF span<Y>.html pages = primary for dates AND scores; span2022..span2025 (Karel Stokkermans pages) carry full round-by-round sections and were parsed fresh with tools/parse_rsssf_spa.py on 2026-08-06 (ledgers spa-<season>-ledger.txt). ADAPTATION for 2025-26: rsssf.org/tabless/span2026.html answers 404 on the return date (verified 2026-08-06; the span2025 page footer links only backward; RSSSF site search has no 2025-26 Spain page) - RSSSF carries NO 2025-26 Spain content at all (no round listings, no final table). The 2025-26 match rows were therefore sourced from the independent index fbref (schedule page, 25 chunks) and are labelled fbref-laliga-2526; the official final table (Wikipedia template sourced from LaLiga EA Sports) is the table authority and the recompute of those 380 rows reproduces it club-for-club and in position order EXACT (gate below). Second-index coverage: Wikipedia FBR matrices diffed for all five seasons (see second_index NOTE); football-data SP1 2526 CSV MD1 block 8/8 identical; worldfootball MD16 page corroborates the postponement. Conflicts were resolved per section-4(3) and are disclosed in the source_conflict NOTE; nothing else in the five seasons diverges between any two indexes.

NOTE|warning|source_conflict|2025-26 MD16 Levante-Villarreal date: the Wikipedia rescheduled-matches register prints "17 February 2026", but TWO independent indexes agree against it - the fbref carrier row carries 2026-02-18 (Wednesday), and OneFootball reports La Liga confirmed the rearranged date as "Wednesday 18 February at 20:00". Per the two-indexes-against-one doctrine the pack row carries 2026-02-18; the Wikipedia print is disclosed here. Score (0-1) and round label (MD16) were never in doubt; the final table is unaffected either way.
